## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear, consistent identity and a sensible route: company overview, margin proof (slides 4 to 6), strategy and targets (7 to 9), capital allocation (10 to 12), then one section per segment, with the reconciliations tucked into appendices. Slide titles are mostly claims, charts use a restrained pink and grey palette that highlights the latest year, and slides 7, 10 and 11 are well composed and readable. The weaknesses are real, though: slide 5 overlays three indexed series on a percentage axis with a separate data table, so the reader cannot tell what the dots mean without effort; the y-axes on slides 4 and 5 start at 10%; and slides 8, 13, 14, 16 and 18 are dense, with small text. The appendix pages (25 to 31) are tiny, low-contrast tables, slide 31 uses a fraction of its page, and the deck ends on reconciliation tables with no closing summary.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the pink-highlighted current-year bars on slides 9 and 10, the strategy and targets layout on slide 7, and the claim-style titles that carry the argument.

**Most useful repair:** Redraw slide 5 so each series has a readable scale or label and the takeaway is stated, and enlarge or reflow the appendix tables on slides 27 to 31 so they are legible.

**What would change the score:** I would add a short closing slide restating the targets, thin the text on the segment overview slides (14, 16, 18), and replace the heavy cycle graphic repeated on slides 1 and 7 with something that carries more information.

_Confidence high. 31 of 31 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This Q1 2026 investor presentation from Owens Corning opens with a title slide listing the investor conferences where it was shared, followed by forward-looking statements and non-GAAP disclaimers. It then introduces the company as a building products leader with three businesses (Roofing, Insulation and Doors), shows revenue by end market and geography, and compares its adjusted EBITDA margin with peers and against housing and shingle market data. Next come margin assumptions by segment, strategic priorities and financial targets through 2028, and capital allocation and capex plans. Segment sections for Roofing, Insulation and Doors follow, covering market position, secular trends and cost initiatives, with appendices holding market data, key financial tables, revenue disaggregation and non-GAAP reconciliations. The deck closes with reconciliation tables rather than a call to action. Slides worth studying include the peer margin comparison on slide 4, which pairs company and peer data on one chart, the margin-versus-macro overlay on slide 5, the strategic priorities and targets layout on slide 7, and the cash flow and capital allocation charts on slides 10 and 11.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Owens Corning Q1 2026 investor presentation have?**

The Owens Corning Q1 2026 investor presentation has 31 pages in total. Slide 1 is the title slide, which lists the investor events where the presentation was shared.

**What financial targets does the Owens Corning presentation set through 2028?**

Slide 7 lists financial targets to outperform the market: $12.5B revenue by 2028, a mid-20% adjusted EBITDA margin sustained, $5B+ 2025-2028 cumulative FCF, and mid-teens+ return on capital. It also lists $2B returned to shareholders 2025-2026.

**What does the Owens Corning deck say about cost synergies in its Doors business?**

Slide 20 states that Owens Corning identified an additional $75 million of synergies above the original $125 million enterprise commitment. Slide 20 also lists network optimization, automation and other efficiency as cost improvement priorities.

**How does Owens Corning's adjusted EBITDA margin compare with peers in its deck?**

Slide 4, titled Profitability Outperforming Our Peers, charts Owens Corning's adjusted EBITDA margin against peers from 2019 to 2025 on one chart. The peers are defined as Carlisle (CSL), Fortune Brands Innovations (FBIN), and MASCO (MAS), and the peer figures are averages.

**Layout**

Left half is white with a left-aligned stacked three-line title at about 18-42% height, a subtitle line below, and a three-row bordered table of events beneath it. The right half (from about 52% of width) is a collage of photographs with a large circular white and grey wheel diagram centred at about 75% x, 51% y. The Owens Corning logo sits at the bottom-left corner.

**Visuals**

Photo collage across the right 48% of the slide, all darkened with a 25% black overlay: top-left a white suburban house with porch, columns and dark windows on a lawn; top-right a stone and brick house with a dark shingle roof; middle-left a wooden-clad entry with a glass-panelled door; middle-right a worker in gloves using a green nail gun on a roof underlayment printed with a Pink Panther outline; bottom-left a white door and a worker in a plaid shirt installing pink FOAMULAR insulation board; bottom-centre a worker in plaid shirt installing pink insulation between wooden roof trusses; bottom-right close-up of pink fibreglass insulation batts. White diagonal lines divide the bottom photos in a triangular wedge. Over the collage is a large white circle with a light grey (#E6E6E6) ring, a magenta circular arrow ring with three arrowheads and curved grey/white labels around it: CONSISTENT EXECUTION on top in magenta, SHAREHOLDER VALUE on the lower left in grey, CUSTOMER GROWTH on the lower right in grey. Inside, the circle is divided by light grey diagonal lines into four quadrants, each with a black line icon above magenta condensed text: top a Pink Panther face icon with ICONIC BRAND; left a pie chart with dollar sign icon with WINNING COST POSITION; right a dumbbell icon with UNPARALLELED COMMERCIAL STRENGTH; bottom a light bulb with gear icon with LEADING TECHNOLOGY. The centre has a white circle with a red Owens Corning logo and black ADVANTAGE™ text. The left table has black 2px outlines with a narrow date column and a wider event column, 3 rows. Red Owens Corning logo with registered mark at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF on the left; photo collage with 25% black overlay on the right 48%. White background on the left with pure black and magenta (#D40F7D) typography. Titles use Oswald Regular in all caps: first line black #000000, next two lines magenta #D40F7D, at about 7.4% of slide height. Subtitle and table are Roboto Light in black, subtitle about 3% of height and table about 2.2%. Thin black table borders with no fills. The photos carry a 25% black tint, and the diagram uses white and light grey (#E6E6E6) with magenta (#D40F7D) accents and condensed Oswald-style labels. No shadows or gradients.

FORWARD-LOOKING STATEMENTS AND NON-GAAP MEASURES

additional changes to tariff, trade or investment policies or laws by the United States, or similar actions, including reciprocal actions, by foreign governments; availability and cost of energy and raw materials; competitive and pricing factors; relationships with key customers and customer concentration in certain areas; our ability to achieve expected synergies, cost reductions and/or productivity improvements; issues related to acquisitions, divestitures and joint ventures or expansions; our ability to complete the announced divestiture of our glass reinforcements business on the expected terms and within the anticipated time period, or at all, which is dependent on the parties' ability to satisfy certain closing conditions; climate change, weather conditions and storm activity; legislation and related regulations or interpretations in the United States or elsewhere; domestic and international economic and political conditions, policies or other governmental actions, as well as war and civil disturbance; uninsured losses or major manufacturing disruptions, including those from natural disasters, catastrophes, pandemics, theft or sabotage; environmental, product-related or other legal and regulatory liabilities, proceedings or actions; research and development activities and intellectual property protection; issues involving implementation and protection of information technology systems; foreign exchange and commodity price fluctuations; our level of indebtedness; our liquidity and the availability and cost of credit; the level of fixed costs required to run our business; levels of goodwill or other indefinite-lived intangible assets; loss of key employees and labor disputes or shortages; defined benefit plan funding obligations; and factors detailed from time to time in the company’s filings with the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission. The information in this presentation speaks as of February 25, 2026, and is subject to change. The company does not undertake any obligation to update or revise any forward-looking statements, whether as a result of new information, future events or otherwise, except as required by federal securities laws.
The term “year to date” refers to the period ended on the last calendar day of the quarter preceding the date of the investor event referenced on the first page above. 3

**Layout**

Title and subtitle top-left over a thin grey rule. Middle band split into three columns by thin vertical grey lines: big revenue figure at left, end-market donut chart with legend in the centre, geography donut chart with legend at right. Below a thin rule is a full-width grey banner heading, then three equal columns each with a grey header box (name and sales), descriptive text on the left and a photo on the right. Footnotes and logo at bottom-left, page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Two donut charts. Revenue by End Market: segments clockwise from top: Non-Discretionary Repair 34% (dark teal #004D65), Residential R&R 17% (blue #009ACA), New Residential 23% (light blue #84E2FF), Non-Residential 26% (pale blue #C1F0FF); white percentage labels inside segments; legend at right with square markers in matching colours. Revenue by Geography: United States 80% (green #3C9748), Canada & Mexico 8% (light green #7FCB88), Europe 11% (pale green #D4EED8), Rest of World 1% (light grey); white labels; legend at right with square markers. Photos: roofing shingle roof and house with brown siding and white windows (right of Roofing column); insulation collage of four stacked images (pink foam board labelled FOAMULAR NGX, grey pipe insulation pieces, gloved hand with tan insulation batt, pink fibreglass insulation); white paneled door with glass panes, dark blue siding, pink flowers and steps (right of Doors column). Owens Corning red logo square with white 'OWENS CORNING' text at bottom-left. Thin grey divider lines and grey header boxes.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title black #000000 in Oswald Light, caps, about 5.9% of slide height; subtitle grey #7F7F7F Roboto Light about 4.4%. Section headings black Oswald Regular caps about 3%. Large revenue figure magenta #D40F7D Oswald SemiBold about 13%. Banner heading magenta #D30F7D Oswald Medium caps. Body text Roboto Light black about 2.6% with key phrases in magenta Roboto Medium. Legend text grey #595959 Roboto Light. Grey boxes #D0CECE, flat, no shadows or rounded corners. Footnotes small black Roboto Light.

### Slide 4

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Plots the company's adjusted EBITDA margin against a peer average on the same axis, with footnotes explaining how the comparison was built.
- Review: The deck has a clear, consistent identity and a sensible route: company overview, margin proof (slides 4 to 6), strategy and targets (7 to 9), capital allocation (10 to 12), then one section per segment, with the reconciliations tucked into appendices.
- Review: The weaknesses are real, though: slide 5 overlays three indexed series on a percentage axis with a separate data table, so the reader cannot tell what the dots mean without effort; the y-axes on slides 4 and 5 start at 10%; and slides 8, 13, 14, 16 and 18 are dense, with small text.

**Layout**

Title top-left in large condensed capitals. A subtitle is centred between two thin grey horizontal rules spanning the content width. The clustered bar chart fills the middle, with the legend at its top right. Footnotes sit bottom-left beside a small logo, and the page number is at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Clustered column chart with 7 year categories (2019 to 2025) and two series. The y-axis runs 10% to 25% with labels at 10%, 15%, 20% and 25%. A black vertical axis line is on the left and a black horizontal baseline sits at the bottom. There are no gridlines and no data labels. Magenta bars are OC, with values of about 18%, 19%, 22%, 23%, 24%, 25% and 22% for 2019 to 2025. Blue bars are Peers, with values of about 17%, 18%, 18%, 20%, 21%, 22% and 21%. The legend at top right has a magenta square labelled OC and a blue square labelled Peers. The Owens Corning logo is at bottom left: a red square with white 'OWENS CORNING' text.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title and subtitle are black in Oswald, a condensed sans-serif; the title is light weight at about 5.9% of slide height and the subtitle is regular at about 3%. Axis labels are grey #7F7F7F (Roboto Light for the y-axis, Oswald for the years). Bars are magenta #D30F7D and blue #009ACA with flat fills and no shadows. Footnotes are small black Roboto Light at about 1.3%. Rules are light grey #C8C8C8 and about 1px thick.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Overlays adjusted EBITDA margin with housing starts, shingle market and existing home sales indexed to a common base year, showing how macro data is layered onto company performance.
- Review: The deck has a clear, consistent identity and a sensible route: company overview, margin proof (slides 4 to 6), strategy and targets (7 to 9), capital allocation (10 to 12), then one section per segment, with the reconciliations tucked into appendices.
- Review: The weaknesses are real, though: slide 5 overlays three indexed series on a percentage axis with a separate data table, so the reader cannot tell what the dots mean without effort; the y-axes on slides 4 and 5 start at 10%; and slides 8, 13, 14, 16 and 18 are dense, with small text.
- Suggested repair: Redraw slide 5 so each series has a readable scale or label and the takeaway is stated, and enlarge or reflow the appendix tables on slides 27 to 31 so they are legible.

**Layout**

Beneath is a bar chart with dots across ten year columns, followed by a three-row data table aligned to the year columns with coloured dot keys at left. Footnotes at bottom left beside the Owens Corning logo; page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Combined bar and dot chart. Ten grey bars (OC adjusted EBITDA margin) for 2016-2025 on a y-axis labelled 10%, 15%, 20%, 25% (no printed bar values). Approximate bar tops: 2016 about 19%, 2017 about 19%, 2018 about 17.8%, 2019 about 17.8%, 2020 about 19%, 2021 about 22%, 2022 about 23%, 2023 about 24%, 2024 about 25%, 2025 about 22%. Overlaid dots per year: green (housing starts), yellow (shingle market), orange (existing home sales), plotted as index positions. Green dots about: 2016 hidden, 2017 about 19%, 2018 about 19.7%, 2019 about 19.2%, 2020 about 20.6%, 2021 about 24.2%, 2022 about 24.8%, 2023 about 21.7%, 2024 about 21.3%, 2025 about 21.2%. Yellow dots about: 2017 19.2%, 2018 18.1%, 2019 18.6%, 2020 20.2%, 2021 21.2%, 2022 19.7%, 2023 20.9%, 2024 21.2%, 2025 19.2%. Orange dots about: 2016 18.1%, 2017 18.2%, 2018 17.8%, 2019 17.8%, 2020 18.7%, 2021 20%, 2022 16.7%, 2023 13.8%, 2024 13.8%, 2025 13.9%. Black vertical y-axis line and black horizontal x-axis line. Legend at top right: grey square with label OC. Left of the table rows are large coloured dots: green, yellow, orange. Red Owens Corning logo (white script 'Owens Corning') at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, minimalist corporate look. Title in black Oswald Light all caps, about 6% of slide height. Subtitle black Oswald, about 3%. **Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear, consistent identity and a sensible route: company overview, margin proof (slides 4 to 6), strategy and targets (7 to 9), capital allocation (10 to 12), then one section per segment, with the reconciliations tucked into appendices.

**Layout**

Title top-left at the margin. Full-width light grey banner below with centered headline. Three equal columns (Roofing, Insulation, Doors), each with a grey header, a photo and a light grey caption box. Centered 'Key macro assumptions' label above four equal columns separated by thin grey rules. Footer has the logo and notes at bottom-left and the page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Three photographs. Roofing: a roofer in a red long-sleeve shirt and tan work trousers kneels on a grey shingled roof and uses a nail gun, in bright sunlight with a hard shadow, cropped box. Insulation: a man in a grey cap and dark shirt pushes pink fibreglass insulation into a wooden wall frame with yellow wiring, warm tones, cropped box. Doors: a woman with a long ponytail in a light green top stands holding a dark picture frame beside a white six-panel door on white horizontal siding, bright and soft. Four thin grey horizontal rules above and below each macro assumption column. Owens Corning red logo with a white script wordmark at the bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in black Oswald Light, caps, about 5.9% of slide height. Magenta #D30F7D Oswald Medium for the banner headline and for macro figures. Black Oswald Medium for range and percent figures, with Roboto Light for descriptive text. Banner and caption fills are #F2F2F2, column headers #CFCFCF. Flat shapes with square corners, no shadows or gradients. Banner has thin grey top and bottom borders.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses a compact three-column layout to pair strategic priorities with headline financial targets, making the goals easy to scan.
- Review: Slide titles are mostly claims, charts use a restrained pink and grey palette that highlights the latest year, and slides 7, 10 and 11 are well composed and readable.
- Worth keeping: Keep the pink-highlighted current-year bars on slides 9 and 10, the strategy and targets layout on slide 7, and the claim-style titles that carry the argument.
- What would change the score: I would add a short closing slide restating the targets, thin the text on the segment overview slides (14, 16, 18), and replace the heavy cycle graphic repeated on slides 1 and 7 with something that carries more information.

**Layout**

White slide with a title top-left. Three columns: left column of strategic priorities (three stacked blocks separated by grey rules), a central circular cycle diagram, and a right column with a 2x2 grid of financial targets plus a bottom $2B line. Owens Corning logo at bottom-left, page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Central circular diagram: a thick light grey ring (about 36% wide, 64% tall) divided by a white X into four quadrants (top, right, bottom, left). Each quadrant has a black line icon with a magenta label: top, a panther face (Iconic Brand); left, a pie chart with a dollar sign (Winning Cost Position); right, a dumbbell in a square (Unparalleled Commercial Strength); bottom, a lightbulb with a gear (Leading Technology). A white circle in the centre with a light grey outline holds the red Owens Corning logo square and the word ADVANTAGE™. Around the ring are three magenta curved arrows with curved magenta uppercase labels: CONSISTENT EXECUTION on top, CUSTOMER GROWTH on the right (rotated along the ring) and SHAREHOLDER VALUE on the left (grey text). Arrowheads point clockwise. Magenta horizontal rules run under STRATEGIC PRIORITIES (left, to the ring) and under the FINANCIAL TARGETS heading (from the ring to the right edge). Light grey horizontal dividers sit between the left priority blocks and under the last one. The right grid has a light grey vertical divider and horizontal dividers. The red Owens Corning logo square is at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Titles are black condensed Oswald, light weight for the main title in uppercase. Section headings are Oswald Regular uppercase black. Key words and big numbers are magenta #D30F7D in Roboto Bold. Body text is black Roboto Light. Ring is light grey #E6E6E6. Lines are magenta, about 0.4% thick, and grey #C8C8C8 for dividers. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 8

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are real, though: slide 5 overlays three indexed series on a percentage axis with a separate data table, so the reader cannot tell what the dots mean without effort; the y-axes on slides 4 and 5 start at 10%; and slides 8, 13, 14, 16 and 18 are dense, with small text.

**Text**

CONSISTENTLY EXECUTING THE OC ADVANTAGE™
IMPROVING MARKET POSITION & CUSTOMER MIX
✓ Contractor engagement model
✓ Pink Advantage Dealer Program
✓ Leading brand creates pull-through opportunities
IMPROVING PRODUCT MIX
✓ Conversion of strips to lams
✓ Growing the Roofing components business
✓ Emphasizing growth in non-residential applications like data centers
INVESTING IN GROWTH
✓ KC fiberglass line to support growth in non-residential and residential
✓ Prattville shingle plant to service largest asphalt shingle region in the U.S.
✓ Russellville plant supports rising commercial market growth
CONTINUOUS OPERATIONAL EFFICIENCY
✓ Doors network optimization; closed 5 facilities
✓ Leverage advanced analytics and AI to drive efficiency
✓ Factory modernization initiative improving cost position
✓ Driving opex leverage through enterprise scale
Organic growth
Higher, more resilient earnings
Lower long-term capital intensity
Robust free cash flow
8

**Layout**

Title top-left across the slide. Four stepped pink header blocks rise left to right, each with a diagonal lower edge and a checkmark bullet list in its own column beneath. A dashed bracket and black arrow circle sit at the right of the columns, leading to four stacked outlined boxes in a right column. Owens Corning logo bottom-left, page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Four magenta (#D30F7D) step-shaped header panels with rounded top-left corners and slanted bottom edges, stepping up in height from left to right. White panels with thin grey vertical divider lines hold the bullet lists. A dashed black bracket opens to the left, from the top of the fourth column down to the bottom, ending in a black filled circle with a white right-pointing arrow. Four white rectangles with magenta outlines on the right. Owens Corning red logo square with white text at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in black Oswald Light uppercase, about 6% of slide height. Column headings in white Oswald uppercase, about 3.7% of slide height, on magenta #D30F7D. Bullets in black Roboto Light, about 2.6% of slide height, with Wingdings checkmarks. Right boxes have 2px magenta #D30F7D outlines, with black Roboto Medium text at about 3.3%. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

**Layout**

Large title top-left. Below, a two-panel section separated by a thin dotted vertical divider at the centre, framed by grey horizontal rules above and below the panel headers. Each panel has a centred header, a two-line centred subtitle, and a six-bar column chart sitting on a baseline. **Visuals**

Two bar charts of six columns each with value labels above the bars and category labels beneath a thin black baseline. Left chart, Revenue ($B): 2015-2020 Average $6.4, 2021 $8.5, 2022 $9.8, 2023 $9.7, 2024 Proforma $10.6, 2025 $10.1. No axes, gridlines or legend. Grey horizontal rules run above and below the panel headers, and a thin dotted vertical line divides the panels. Owens Corning logo: a red square with white 'OWENS CORNING' text, at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in black, Oswald Light, all caps, about 5.9% of slide height. Panel headers in black Oswald Regular caps at 3.3%. Subtitles and chart labels in Roboto Light black. Accent magenta #D40F7D in Roboto Medium for 'CAGR of ~4%' and 'Sustainable 20%+', and for the 2025 bars. Grey bars #D0CECE. Grey rules #BFBFBF. Flat design with no shadows or gradients and square-cornered bars.

**Layout**

Title top-left, left-aligned, with a wide empty band beneath. Below it a two-column panel divided by a thin dotted vertical line at the centre, each column with a centred header, a centred subtitle and a bar chart. Footer at bottom-left holds the logo with note and source lines, and the page number sits at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Two side-by-side vertical bar charts with no y-axis, no gridlines and no legend, each on a thin black baseline. Left chart, Operating Cash Flow ($B), has six bars with value labels above them: 2015-2020 Average $0.9, 2021 $1.5, 2022 $1.8, 2023 $1.7, 2024 $1.9, 2025 $1.8. The first five bars are light grey and the 2025 bar is magenta. Right chart, Free Cash Flow ($B), has six bars: 2015-2020 Average $0.5, 2021 $1.1, 2022 $1.3, 2023 $1.2, 2024 $1.2, 2025 $1.0. The first five bars are light grey and the 2025 bar is magenta. Light grey horizontal rules run above and below the column headers across the full panel width. A thin dotted vertical divider runs between the two charts. The Owens Corning logo, a small red square with white 'OWENS CORNING' text, sits at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide. The title and headers are black Oswald in uppercase, with a Light weight for the title and Regular for the headers. Body text is black Roboto Light. Accent text is magenta #D40F7D in Roboto Medium. Grey bars are #D0CECE and the highlighted bars are magenta #D40F7D. Rules are light grey #C8C8C8 and thin. There are no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. The title is about 6% of the slide height, headers about 3.3%, subtitles about 3%, chart labels about 2.2% and footnotes about 1.3%.

**Layout**

Title top-left across the slide. Below it two bordered panels side by side, each with a grey header band: left panel (about 46% wide) holds four stacked text statements separated by thin grey rules; right panel (about 44% wide) holds a stacked bar chart. A full-width magenta rule sits below, with three centred stat callouts under it separated by thin vertical grey lines. Logo bottom-left, page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart 'Free cash flow allocation' with six columns, categories 2015–2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025, no y-axis or value labels, black baseline. Each column stacks Share Repurchases (blue #009ACA) at bottom, Dividend (green #3C9748) in the middle, Other (light grey #D0CECE) on top, all columns equal total height. Approximate segment proportions: 2015–2020 blue about 33%, green about 16%, grey about 51%; 2021 blue about 57%, green about 12%, grey about 31%; 2022 blue about 61%, green about 10%, grey about 29%; 2023 blue about 56%, green about 16%, grey about 28%; 2024 blue about 38%, green about 17%, grey about 45%; 2025 blue about 80%, green about 20%, no grey. A horizontal dashed magenta line at about the 50% level spans all columns, ending in a black left-pointing triangle arrowhead next to the annotation '50% FCF to shareholders target over time', followed by a thin black rule and '$2B return to shareholders 2025-2026'. Legend below the chart with square swatches: blue Share Repurchases, green Dividend, grey Other. Owens Corning red logo with white 'OWENS CORNING' wordmark at bottom-left. Thin grey divider lines between left-panel statements and thin magenta full-width rule.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, minimal corporate style. Title in black condensed light sans (Oswald Light) all caps, about 5.9% of slide height. Panel headers in Oswald Regular caps, black on light grey #E6E6E6 bands with thin grey borders. Body text in Roboto Light about 3.3% height, black, with key phrases highlighted in magenta #D40F7D. Chart labels Oswald Regular and Roboto Light about 2.2%. Magenta #D40F7D rule and dashed line. No shadows or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Full-width white slide. Condensed all-caps title at top left. Thin grey horizontal rules at about y=17% and y=66% span the margins. A timeline curve occupies the middle band with labels placed around it. A row of four equal-sized photos sits along the lower part, aligned to the left and right margins. Logo at bottom left, page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

A thick black S-shaped curve rises from lower left (near the 5% label) to a peak, then falls and flattens out to the right, ending in a black arrowhead pointing right toward the 4% label. Three small white circles with black outlines mark points on the curve: one on the rise (2024), one at the peak (2025-2027), one on the descent (2028). Thin black dashed vertical lines drop from or rise to each circle toward its label. Two thin grey horizontal rules frame the diagram. Four photos in a row: (1) people in hard hats and orange hi-vis vests with shovels breaking ground in front of a 'HÄLLEKIS PLANT DECARBONIZATION' sign, cloudy sky, dirt mound in foreground; (2) aerial view of a long white-roofed industrial building with a green landscape and a road behind; (3) a worker in a grey uniform, cap, safety glasses and blue face mask giving a thumbs-up with a green glove, with yellow industrial railings and machinery blurred behind; (4) interior of a factory with a conveyor line carrying pink insulation material, yellow safety rails and machinery. Owens Corning red logo with white script text at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title in black #000000, Oswald Light, all caps, about 5.9% of slide height. Year labels in black Oswald Regular about 3.7%. Descriptions in black Roboto Light about 2.6%. Large percentage figures in magenta #D40F7D, Oswald SemiBold, about 6.7%, with Roboto Light 3.3% captions. Grey rules #AAAAAA. Photos have square corners with no shadow. Curve stroke about 0.5% of slide height in black.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are real, though: slide 5 overlays three indexed series on a percentage axis with a separate data table, so the reader cannot tell what the dots mean without effort; the y-axes on slides 4 and 5 start at 10%; and slides 8, 13, 14, 16 and 18 are dense, with small text.

**Text**

LEADING TECHNOLOGY & SUSTAINABILITY POSITION
PRODUCT AND PROCESS INNOVATION
DIGITAL TOOLS TO HELP OUR / CUSTOMERS WIN AND GROW
SUSTAINABILITY IMPACT
BUILDING BETTER TOGETHER 2024 SUSTAINABILITY REPORT
Customer-inspired product innovation
~120 new or improved products / launched in last 3 years
Maintained 20%+ Product Vitality Index / in 2025
Productivity driven process innovation
Contractor selling tools
VR technology for contractor training
Digital customer portal
Self-guided online customer / learning modules
Increase the positive impact of our / products
Reduce the negative impact of our / operations
Eliminate injuries and improve the / quality of life of our employees and their / families
Have a positive impact on our / communities
13

**Layout**

Title top left. Three equal columns under a full-width light grey header band, each with a centered header, a photo strip, and centered text lines below. Thin grey vertical dividers separate the columns. Owens Corning logo bottom left, page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three photo strips under the headers. Left: close-up of a handheld thermal imaging camera (TG165-X, showing 447°F with a rainbow heat map) held against industrial piping, blurred background, blue and orange tones, cropped box. Middle: a man in a pink shirt wearing a VR headset in a warehouse stacked with pink insulation bags, with a glowing teal AR overlay, and a monitor image in the lower left showing a web page with a brick house with trees. Right: black banner reading BUILDING BETTER TOGETHER in white and magenta with 2024 SUSTAINABILITY REPORT in white spaced capitals, faint figures in pink at the top corners. Owens Corning logo: red square with white script text.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in black Oswald Light, all caps, about 6% of slide height. Column headers in black Oswald Regular caps on light grey #E6E6E6 band. Body text centered Roboto Light black with key phrases in Roboto Medium magenta #D40F7D, about 3% of slide height. Thin grey divider lines, no shadows.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are real, though: slide 5 overlays three indexed series on a percentage axis with a separate data table, so the reader cannot tell what the dots mean without effort; the y-axes on slides 4 and 5 start at 10%; and slides 8, 13, 14, 16 and 18 are dense, with small text.

14

**Layout**

Title top-left. Left column (about 3% to 48% width) holds a row of four magenta-labelled photo tiles under the title, followed by five stacked text statements separated by thin grey rules. Right half holds a combo bar/line chart at top, a thin horizontal rule, and two donut charts side by side below, split by a thin vertical rule. Footer notes at bottom left beside the Owens Corning logo; page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Four photo tiles, each with a magenta label bar on top: SHINGLES (close-up of dark brown/grey asphalt shingles), COMPONENTS (a roofer in tan work pants and boots walking on a dark grey roof underlayment), ASPHALT (industrial refinery tanks and pipes, blue sky), NONWOVENS (white/grey nonwoven fabric roll, soft light). Combo chart: blue bars of revenue ($b) for 2021 to 2025 with values $3.2, $3.7, $4.0, $4.6, $4.4 printed in white inside the bars; left axis $0.0 to $4.5 in $1.5 steps; magenta line for EBITDA margin on right axis 0% to 35% in 5% steps, about 20% in 2021, about 19% in 2022, about 26% in 2023, about 30% in 2024, about 28% in 2025; legend with blue square for Revenue ($b) and magenta line for EBITDA margin (%). Donut chart Revenue by Product: Shingles about 70% dark burgundy #7D0049, Components about 30% magenta #D40F7D, white thin separators. Donut chart Revenue by Geography: U.S. about 90% dark green #1E4B24, Europe about 5% mid green, Canada & Mexico about 2% light green, with labels outside. Red Owens Corning logo at bottom left. Thin grey divider lines between statements, a horizontal rule under the chart, and a vertical rule between donuts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and headings in black #000000 Oswald, light for main title (about 6% of slide height), regular for section headings (about 3.3%). Body statements mix Oswald Regular uppercase bold-lead phrases with Roboto Light sentence text, about 3% height, black. Accents magenta #D40F7D, blue #009ACA, burgundy #7D0049, dark green #1E4B24. Tile labels white uppercase Oswald on magenta. Flat shapes, no shadows or rounded corners, thin light grey rules.

15

**Layout**

Large title at top left. Below, three equal-width columns, each with a bordered header box with centered title, a chart panel, and a statistic row at the bottom with a big magenta number on the left and descriptive text on the right, between thin grey rules. Footer has Owens Corning logo at bottom left, footnote beside it, and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left: bar chart of OCCN members, 11 grey vertical bars rising from '15 to '25, with a magenta line with markers (Duration Mix) rising from about 20% to about 60% of height, overlaid; legend at top left with a magenta line-with-dot and a grey square; callout '>30,000 CONTRACTORS' in magenta above the last bar; no gridlines, thin grey baseline. Middle: stacked column chart of U.S. asphalt shingle market in MM squares, 6 columns (2015-2020 AVG., 2021-2025), stacked grey (new construction) at the bottom, blue (repair) in the middle, green (weather) on top. Totals: 136, 159, 148, 157, 160, 143 (2025 in bold magenta). Segments, grey/blue/green: avg 21/75/40; 2021 28/90/41; 2022 26/81/41; 2023 24/78/55; 2024 25/81/54; 2025 23/79/41. Legend at top with grey, green and blue squares. Black baseline. Right: 100% stacked columns, 6 columns, green laminates at the bottom with a white diagonal axis-break slash and grey strips on top. Grey share about 38% for the avg, about 28% for 2021, about 20% for 2022-2023, about 15% for 2024-2025 (estimates). Legend with a green square for Laminates and a grey square for Strips. Owens Corning pink/red logo square at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles in black condensed Oswald, light-weight uppercase main title. Magenta #D40F7D accents for big stats and callouts. Greys #AFABAB, #7F7F7F, #959595; green #3C9748; blue #009ACA. Body text in Roboto Light black. Panels have thin light grey borders, with a grey line under the header. Flat, no shadows or gradients. Title about 6% of the slide height; body about 2.6%.

**Layout**

White slide. Title top-left. Left column (about 3% to 47%) holds three magenta header tabs with photos beneath, then four stacked heading+body statements separated by thin grey rules. Right column holds a bar-and-line chart at top, a thin horizontal rule, then two donut charts side by side separated by a thin vertical rule. Footnotes run along the bottom left beside a corner logo; page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Combo chart 'Financial Performance': blue columns for Revenue ($b) 2021 $3.2, 2022 $3.7, 2023 $3.7, 2024 $3.9, 2025 $3.7, with white value labels inside bars. Left axis $0.0 to $4.0 in $1.0 steps, right axis 0% to 25% in 5% steps. Magenta line for EBITDA margin (%) with no labels, about 20% in 2021 rising to about 22% in 2022, about 22% in 2023, about 24% in 2024, about 23% in 2025. Legend above: blue square 'Revenue ($b)' and magenta line 'EBITDA margin (%)'. Thin grey baseline. Three photos beneath magenta tabs: N.A. Residential, a family of three adults and a child standing in an unfinished room with pink insulation in the walls and a ladder; N.A. Non-Residential, a modern glass-and-steel building with a slanted canopy and bare trees; Europe, an aerial view of a rooftop green terrace with plants and buildings. Donut 'Revenue by Product': about 40% NA Residential (dark plum #7D0049), about 40% NA Non-Residential (magenta #D30F7D), about 20% Europe (light pink #FFBAE2). Donut 'Revenue by Geography': about 68% U.S. (dark green #1E4B24), about 20% Europe (mid green #3C9748), about 12% Canada & Mexico (light green #7FCC89). Owens Corning logo, a red square with white 'OWENS CORNING' script, at bottom left. Thin grey divider rules between text blocks, a horizontal rule above donuts and a vertical rule between donuts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles in black #000000 condensed Oswald, uppercase; main title Oswald Light about 5.9% of slide height; section headings Oswald Regular about 3% to 3.3%. Body text black Roboto Light about 3% of slide height. Accents magenta #D40F7D with white Oswald text in tabs. Chart blue #009ACA. Footnotes tiny Roboto Light about 1.3%. Flat style, no shadows, no rounded corners, thin light-grey lines #CCCCCC.

**Layout**

White slide with a left-aligned title at top. Below it, three equal columns (each about 31% wide) run edge to edge from x 3.8% to 96.2%. Each column has a photo on top, a light grey caption band with centered text, then two stat rows with magenta figures on the left and descriptions on the right. Thin grey vertical dividers separate the columns in the stat area. Footnotes and a logo sit at bottom left, and the page number sits at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three photographs side by side, each 30.8% wide and 27.2% tall, at y 18.7%. 1) Left: interior of a house under construction with pink fiberglass batt insulation between wooden studs and rafters. A man in a cap and dark polo, a man holding a baby or toddler, and a woman in a light blue shirt and jeans stand looking up. A stepladder and loose pink insulation rolls are at the left. Warm light, pink and brown tones. 2) Middle: low-angle view of a modern commercial building with a large overhanging solar panel canopy roof and tall glass windows with vertical white fins. Bare tree branches are in the foreground, a blue sky behind, and part of a brick building at right. Cool blue and white tones. 3) Right: aerial drone view of a rooftop terrace garden with a maze-like pattern of green planting beds, grey paving tiles and white walls, with a few people gathered at the top. Green and grey tones. Under the photos are three light grey rectangles (#E6E6E6) holding the column headings. Two thin grey vertical divider lines run in the lower stat area, at about x 34.6% and x 65.4%. The Owens Corning logo, a small red square with white 'OWENS CORNING' script, is at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. The title is in black Oswald Light, all caps, about 5.9% of slide height. Column headings are black Oswald Regular caps at about 3.3%. Sub-captions and descriptions are black Roboto Light at 3.0% and 2.6%. Key figures are magenta #D40F7D Oswald SemiBold caps at about 5.2%. Footnotes are tiny Roboto Light at 1.3%. Grey caption bands are #E6E6E6 with no outline, shadow or rounded corners.

### Slide 18

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are real, though: slide 5 overlays three indexed series on a percentage axis with a separate data table, so the reader cannot tell what the dots mean without effort; the y-axes on slides 4 and 5 start at 10%; and slides 8, 13, 14, 16 and 18 are dense, with small text.

**Text**

DOORS SEGMENT OVERVIEW
A DIFFERENTIATED MANUFACTURER OF INNOVATIVE DOORS AND DOOR SYSTEMS in attractive categories
DRIVING A WINNING COST POSITION by exceeding $125 million of original enterprise synergies and identifying $75 million additional; total structural improvement of 500bps
VERTICALLY INTEGRATED across components, panels, and systems enables network flexibility
CREATING VALUE FOR CUSTOMERS THROUGH QUALITY, SERVICE, AND BRAND to drive growth in every channel
STRUCTURALLY IMPROVING the business with a path to 20% EBITDA margins leveraging The OC Advantage™, and positioning to outperform the market
RESIDENTIAL INTERIOR
RESIDENTIAL EXTERIOR
LUXURY EXTERIOR
COMPONENTS
REVENUE BY PRODUCT
REVENUE BY GEOGRAPHY
Components
Interior
Exterior
Canada & Mexico
Europe
U.S.
Source: Owens Corning management estimates, Owens Corning SEC filings; Business mix statistics based on 2025 net sales; estimated error margin +/- 5% for revenue split.
Note: Rest of world (not shown), accounts for <1% of revenue.
18

**Layout**

Title top-left. Left column (about 3.5% to 45% wide) holds five stacked statements separated by thin light-grey horizontal rules. Right side has a row of four magenta header tiles with photos beneath, and below them two donut charts separated by a thin vertical grey line. Footer has an Owens Corning logo at bottom-left, source and note lines beside it, and the page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Four magenta (#D40F7D) header tiles with white labels, each over a product image: a teal-grey interior panel door in a bathroom with a wooden vanity; a white multi-pane exterior door with a dark navy wall and pink flowers; a luxury modern house with a wood-beamed overhanging roof, forested hillside and glass walls; a product render of a door component frame on white. Donut chart 'Revenue by Product' with segments: Interior about 50% dark maroon #7D0049, Exterior about 38% magenta #D40F7D, Components about 12% light pink #FFBAE2; white separators, labels outside. Donut chart 'Revenue by Geography' with segments: U.S. about 80% dark green #1E4B24, Europe about 10% mid green #3C9748, Canada & Mexico about 10% pale green #D4EED8; labels outside. Thin vertical grey divider between the charts. Thin grey horizontal dividers between left statements. Owens Corning red logo with white text at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and statements in black #000000; the bold lead-in phrases are in condensed uppercase Oswald, the rest in Roboto Light. Accents are magenta #D40F7D with white Oswald uppercase labels. The chart colours are a magenta family and a green family. Title is large, about 6% of slide height; body about 3%. Flat design, square corners, no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

White slide with a left-aligned title at top. Below it are two side-by-side bordered panels, each with a light grey header band: a waterfall-style bar chart on the left and four spaced text statements on the right. Footer has an Owens Corning logo at bottom left with a footnote beside it, and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Horizontal waterfall chart with no value axis. A thin black vertical axis line sits at x about 17.3%, running from y about 29% to 80%. Category labels on the left, right-aligned to the axis: Network Optimization, Automation, Other Efficiency, Total. Three grey (#959595) floating bars step down from upper left to lower right. Network Optimization is a short bar from the axis to about 25% x. Automation runs from about 25% to 36.7% x. Other Efficiency runs from about 36.7% to 47% x. Thin dashed vertical connector lines join the end of each bar to the start of the next, and the last one runs down to the Total bar. The Total bar is magenta (#D30F7D), runs from the axis to about 47% x, and has the bold label '75' just to its right. Bar values are not printed, except the total of 75. Panels have thin light grey outlines. The red Owens Corning logo (a red square with a white swoosh and the text OWENS CORNING) sits at the bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate look on a white background. The title is a condensed light sans-serif (Oswald Light) in black #000000, in capitals, about 5.9% of slide height. Panel headers are Oswald Regular capitals in black on light grey bands (#E6E6E6), about 3.3% of slide height. Body text is Roboto Light in black at about 3.3% of slide height, with key phrases (network optimization, automation, efficiency) in magenta #D40F7D. Chart labels are Roboto Light at about 2.2% of slide height. Flat fills, no shadows, no gradients, square corners. Panels have thin #E0E0E0 borders.

22

**Layout**

Title top left. Two side-by-side bordered chart panels (left and right) separated by a thin vertical gray divider at about x 49%. Each panel has a header with title and units, a legend, and a stacked column chart. A percentage table sits beneath each panel. Footnotes run along the bottom left beside a corner logo; the page number is at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two stacked column charts, six columns each (2015-2020 AVG., 2021-2025), values in MM squares, no y-axis, a thin black baseline. Segments from bottom: New Construction gray, Repair blue, Weather green, and in the right chart Components Shingles yellow. Left chart totals: 136, 159, 148, 157, 160, 143. Left chart segments by column, bottom to top: New Construction 21, 28, 26, 24, 25, 23; Repair 75, 90, 81, 78, 81, 79; Weather 40, 41, 41, 55, 54, 41. Right chart totals: 143, 169, 158, 169, 172, 154. Right chart segments: New Construction 21, 28, 26, 24, 25, 23; Repair 75, 90, 81, 78, 81, 79; Weather 40, 41, 41, 55, 54, 41; Components 8, 10, 10, 12, 13, 11. The 2025 totals (143 left, 154 right) are bold magenta. Legends have small square markers: gray New Construction, green Weather, blue Repair, yellow Components Shingles. Two data tables below with no gridlines: left has columns Year, New Construction, Repair, Weather; right adds Components Shingles. Owens Corning logo, a red square with white script, at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in black Oswald Light, large, all caps, about 5.9% of slide height. Panel titles in black Oswald Regular about 3% with a thin light gray panel border and a gray rule under the header. Chart values in white Oswald Light about 2.2%. Axis labels in gray #7F7F7F Oswald. Table and legend text in black Roboto Light about 1.7-2.2%; table headers bold. Chart colours: gray #7F7F7F, blue #009ACA, green #3C9748, yellow #F5BC24, magenta #D40F7D. No shadows or gradients; flat fills.

**Layout**

Title at top left, left-aligned. Two stacked tables of identical structure centred horizontally (about 10% to 90% width), the upper quarterly table at about 23–51% of height and the lower full-year table at about 53–85%. Footnote text at bottom left beside a small logo, page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two tables, each with 9 columns (label column plus four paired groups) and 5 rows: a group header row (Owens Corning*, Roofing, Insulation, Doors), a column header row, and three data rows. Header rows are dark grey with white text. Data rows alternate light grey (Net sales, EBITDA* as % of net sales) and white (EBITDA*). Thin dark vertical dividers separate the label column and each group. Upper table, quarterly: Net sales 2,142/2,574, 774/1,059, 916/987, 486/564; EBITDA* 362/570, 199/338, 186/228, 33/82; EBITDA* as % of net sales 17%/22%, 26%/32%, 20%/23%, 7%/15%. Lower table, full-year 2025/2024: Net sales 10,103/9,851, 4,437/4,630, 3,700/3,926, 2,125/1,448; EBITDA* 2,268/2,468, 1,411/1,532, 848/945, 232/232; EBITDA* as % of net sales 22%/25%, 32%/33%, 23%/24%, 11%/16%. Owens Corning logo (red square with white 'OWENS CORNING' text) at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title in black Oswald Light, uppercase, about 5.9% of slide height. Table header fill dark grey #515151 with white Roboto Medium/Bold text at 2.4% of height. Data rows use Roboto Light black text at 2.4%, with fills #D9D9D9 and white alternating. Footnotes Roboto Light black at 1.3%. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Large left-aligned title across the top. Two stacked financial tables (three months on top, twelve months below) centred, spanning about x 12.5% to 87.5%, each with a dark grey header block, banded rows, and a thick black rule beneath. Footer has a logo at bottom left with a source line next to it, and the slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two data tables. Each has a dark grey (#515151) header: a blank top-left cell and a merged cell reading the period title and '($ in millions)' centred. Below is a column header row: Disaggregation Categories, Roofing, Insulation, Doors, Eliminations, Consolidated. Seven body rows with alternating light grey (#D9D9D9) and white bands: North America Residential (grey), North America Non-Residential (white), Total North America (grey, label indented), Europe (white), Asia-Pacific (grey), Rest of world (white), NET SALES (white, bold). Dollar signs sit left-aligned in the first and last rows of each column and numbers are right-aligned. Thin black rules sit under North America Non-Residential and above and below NET SALES. A thick black bar sits below each table (about y 52% and y 92.5%). Three-month values: NA Residential 625/381/427/(32)/1,401; NA Non-Res 101/343/-/(2)/442; Total NA 726/724/427/(34)/1,843; Europe 47/189/55/-/291; Asia-Pacific 1/-/-/-/1; Rest of world -/3/4/-/7; NET SALES 774/916/486/(34)/2,142. Twelve-month values: 3,791/1,483/1,877/(146)/7,005; 437/1,420/-/(9)/1,848; 4,228/2,903/1,877/(155)/8,853; 200/728/228/(4)/1,152; 9/56/3/-/68; -/13/17/-/30; 4,437/3,700/2,125/(159)/10,103. Owens Corning logo at bottom left: small red square with white 'OWENS CORNING' wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in black #000000 in Oswald Light, all caps, about 5.9% of slide height. Table headers are white Roboto Medium on #515151; body is black Roboto Light at about 1.84% of slide height, with the NET SALES rows in Roboto Bold. Grey banding #D9D9D9. Black rules. Source line is small Roboto Light, about 1.3%. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.
